      /* iOS Safari auto-zooms the whole page when a form field with
         font-size < 16px is focused (header search, size inputs, clan
         search, etc.). Appending maximum-scale=1 to the viewport disables
         that auto-zoom; since iOS 10 Safari ignores maximum-scale for
         USER pinch-zoom, so accessibility zoom still works. Applied via
         JS for iOS only — Android Chrome honors maximum-scale and would
         lose pinch-zoom if we put it in the meta tag directly. The
         'Mac + touch' test catches iPads, which report a Mac user agent. */
      (function () {
        var isIOS = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent) ||
          (/Mac/.test(navigator.userAgent) && 'ontouchend' in document);
        if (!isIOS) return;
        var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="viewport"]');
        if (meta && meta.content.indexOf('maximum-scale') === -1) {
          meta.content += ',maximum-scale=1';
        }
      })();
    </script>

<div id="sks-ann-sections--21917738434774__announcement-bar" class="sks-ann sks-ann--rotate" role="region" aria-label="Site announcements" data-sks-announcement="" data-dismiss-key="sks-ann-dismissed-sections--21917738434774__announcement-bar" data-rotate="" data-rotate-speed="4"><div class="sks-ann__track" role="list"><div class="sks-ann__slide" role="listitem" aria-hidden="true"><span class="sks-ann__item"><p>Wedding party?         <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">

  <script>
    (function () {
      var bar = document.getElementById('sks-ann-sections--21917738434774__announcement-bar');
      if (!bar) return;
      var key = bar.dataset.dismissKey;
      var remember = true;

      if (remember && key && sessionStorage.getItem(key) === '1') {
        bar.style.display = 'none';
      }

      var close = bar.querySelector('.sks-ann__close');
      if (close) {
        close.addEventListener('click', function () {
          bar.style.display = 'none';
          if (remember && key) {
            try { sessionStorage.setItem(key, '1'); } catch (e) {}
          }
        });
      }

      /* Rotate mode: cycle one slide at a time. Skips entirely when the
         viewer prefers reduced motion — they see the first slide, static. */
      if (bar.hasAttribute('data-rotate')) {
        var slides = bar.querySelectorAll('.sks-ann__slide');
        var reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
        if (slides.length > 1 && !reduce) {
          var i = 0;
          var secs = parseFloat(bar.getAttribute('data-rotate-speed')) || 4;
          setInterval(function () {
            slides[i].removeAttribute('data-active');
            slides[i].setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
            i = (i + 1) % slides.length;
            slides[i].setAttribute('data-active', '');
            slides[i].setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
          }, secs * 1000);
        }
      }
    })();
  </script>

            <script type="application/json" data-sks-tartan-meta="">{"currency":"$"}</script><script>
            (function () {
              var form = document.currentScript.closest('section').querySelector('[data-sks-tartan-search]');
              if (!form || form._sksWired) return;
              form._sksWired = true;
              var input = form.querySelector('[data-sks-tartan-input]');
              var dropdown = form.querySelector('[data-sks-tartan-dropdown]');
              var dataEl = form.parentElement.querySelector('[data-sks-tartan-clans]');
              var metaEl = form.parentElement.querySelector('[data-sks-tartan-meta]');
              var pageBase = form.dataset.clanPageBase || '/pages/';
              var clans = [];
              var meta = { currency: '$' };
              try { clans = dataEl ? JSON.parse(dataEl.textContent) : []; }
              catch (e) { console.warn('[SKS-TARTAN-SEARCH] clans JSON parse failed:', e, 'raw:', dataEl && dataEl.textContent && dataEl.textContent.slice(0, 200)); clans = []; }
              try { meta = metaEl ? JSON.parse(metaEl.textContent) : meta; } catch (e) {}
              console.log('[SKS-TARTAN-SEARCH] loaded clans:', clans.length);

              function normalize(s) {
                s = String(s || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                s = s.replace(/^m['’]?c/, 'mac');
                return s;
              }
              clans.forEach(function (c) { c._n = normalize(c.t); });

              function close() { dropdown.hidden = true; dropdown.innerHTML = ''; input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }
              function escapeHtml(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (ch) { return ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' })[ch]; }); }

              function render(query) {
                var q = normalize(query);
                if (!q || q.length < 2) { close(); return; }
                var matches = clans.filter(function (c) { return c._n.indexOf(q) !== -1; });
                matches.sort(function (a, b) {
                  var ai = a._n.indexOf(q) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
                  var bi = b._n.indexOf(q) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
                  if (ai !== bi) return ai - bi;
                  return a.t.localeCompare(b.t);
                });
                matches = matches.slice(0, 8);
                if (!matches.length) {
                  dropdown.innerHTML = '<div class="sks-tartan__dd-empty">No clans match <strong>' + escapeHtml(query) + '</strong>. Press Enter to search the full directory.</div>';
                } else {
                  dropdown.innerHTML = matches.map(function (c) {
                    var price = c.p
                      ? '<span class="sks-tartan__dd-price">From ' + escapeHtml(meta.currency || '$') + Math.round(parseFloat(c.p)) + '/yard</span>'
                      : '';
                    return '<a class="sks-tartan__dd-item" role="option" href="' + pageBase + encodeURIComponent(c.h) + '">'
                      + '<span class="sks-tartan__dd-body">'
                      +   '<span class="sks-tartan__dd-title">' + escapeHtml(c.t) + '</span>'
                      +   price
                      + '</span>'
                      + '</a>';
                  }).join('');
                }
                dropdown.hidden = false;
                input.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
              }

              var t = null;
              input.addEventListener('input', function () {
                clearTimeout(t);
                t = setTimeout(function () { render(input.value); }, 120);
              });
              input.addEventListener('focus', function () { if (input.value.length >= 2) render(input.value); });
              document.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (!form.contains(e.target)) close(); });
              input.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') { close(); input.blur(); }
              });
            })();
          </script><div class="sks-tartan__stats"><div class="sks-tartan__stat">

    <span class="sks-srr__stars-fg" data-srr-fg="" style="width: 84.4%;">★★★★★</span>
  </span>
  <span class="sks-srr__num"><strong data-srr-avg="">4.2</strong> out of 5</span>

<script>
(function () {
  var root = document.getElementById('sks-srr-template--21917738074326__sks_store_rating_mqedfB');
  if (!root || root.dataset.srrDone) return;
  root.dataset.srrDone = '1';

  var store  = root.getAttribute('data-srr-store');
  var apikey = root.getAttribute('data-srr-apikey');

  function word(avg) {
    if (avg >= 4.3) return 'Excellent';
    if (avg >= 3.7) return 'Great';
    if (avg >= 2.9) return 'Good';
    if (avg >= 2.0) return 'Fair';
    return 'Poor';
  }
  function paint(avg, count) {
    if (!avg || !count) { root.style.display = 'none'; return; }
    var w = root.querySelector('[data-srr-word]');
    var fg = root.querySelector('[data-srr-fg]');
    var a = root.querySelector('[data-srr-avg]');
    var c = root.querySelector('[data-srr-count]');
    if (w)  w.textContent = word(avg);
    if (fg) fg.style.width = (Math.max(0, Math.min(5, avg)) / 5 * 100) + '%';
    if (a)  a.textContent = (Math.round(avg * 10) / 10).toFixed(1);
    if (c)  c.textContent = count.toLocaleString('en-US');
    root.setAttribute('data-srr-ready', 'true');
  }

  /* Reviews.io timeline — COMBINED company + product aggregate (matches the
     Reviews.io badge / hero). stats = combined; storeStats = company-only
     fallback. Uses the .myshopify.com handle the timeline endpoint expects
     (from <body data-sks-rio-store-mysfy>); no API key needed here. */
  var mysfy = (document.body && document.body.getAttribute('data-sks-rio-store-mysfy')) || store;
  if (mysfy) {
    fetch('https://api.reviews.io/timeline/data?per_page=1&type=store_review' +
          '&include_local_reviews=false&include_product_reviews=true' +
          '&sort=date_desc&lang=en&store=' + encodeURIComponent(mysfy))
      .then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })
      .then(function (d) {
        var stats = d && (d.stats || d.storeStats);
        if (!stats) { root.style.display = 'none'; return; }
        paint(parseFloat(stats.average_rating), parseInt(stats.review_count, 10));
      })
      .catch(function () { root.style.display = 'none'; });
  } else {
    root.style.display = 'none';
  }
})();
</script>

<section id="sks-story-template--21917738074326__sks_story_xhnnc4" class="sks-story" aria-labelledby="sks-story-template--21917738074326__sks_story_xhnnc4-heading">
  <div class="sks-story__wrap">

    <div class="sks-story__img-wrap"><img src="//kilts.fr/cdn/shop/files/Untitled_design_2_ccb37701-26d0-401d-93fc-83c2d6b698de.jpg?v=1786011329&amp;width=800" alt="Scottish Kilts On Sale" srcset="//kilts.fr/cdn/shop/files/Untitled_design_2_ccb37701-26d0-401d-93fc-83c2d6b698de.jpg?v=1786011329&amp;width=400 400w, //kilts.fr/cdn/shop/files/Untitled_design_2_ccb37701-26d0-401d-93fc-83c2d6b698de.jpg?v=1786011329&amp;width=600 600w, //kilts.fr/cdn/shop/files/Untitled_design_2_ccb37701-26d0-401d-93fc-83c2d6b698de.jpg?v=1786011329&amp;width=800 800w" width="800" height="1004" loading="lazy" sizes="(max-width: 960px) 100vw, 50vw">
</div>

    <div class="sks-story__text"><p class="sks-story__eyebrow">Our story</p><h2 id="sks-story-template--21917738074326__sks_story_xhnnc4-heading" class="sks-story__heading">
          <p>We own the workshop</p><p>That is the whole story</p>
        </h2><div class="sks-story__p">
              <p>Since 2012 we have done something most kilt brands will not. We make every single piece custom to order. There are no warehouses of pre-cut kilts in standard sizes and no "we'll send you the closest fit." Every kilt, shirt, jacket, sporran and waistcoat is made to measure for the person who ordered it.</p><p>We do that because we own our workshops outright. Our own makers cut, hand-pleat and stitch every order, and 98% of them have been with us since we started. Owning the whole process is why we can offer 5,000+ tartans, hold 700+ in Quick Ship stock, and price a first kilt as a realistic purchase rather than a once-in-a-lifetime splurge.</p><p>We are a premier member of KiltGuide.com, the independent Highland-wear directory. Throughout the year we give away free custom kilts to the Scottish-American community, our way of helping keep the heritage alive. <a href="https://scottishkiltshop.com/pages/about-us" data-nf="1">Read our full story</a> →</p>
            </div><div class="sks-story__evidence">
              <p>Today we offer <strong>5,000+ tartans</strong>, carry <strong>10,000+ verified reviews</strong>, and have spent <strong>14+ years</strong> custom-making Highland dress for the diaspora.</p>
            </div></div>
  </div>
</section>

If I had realized that I think I would’ve bothered customer service less (because I was thinking I was waiting on an international tracking number and my order was going to take too long to get here and then we wouldn’t have it to wear).

</footer><script>
    /* Accordion mode — close all <details> columns on phones so the footer
       starts compact. Reopens them on desktop so the layout looks like the
       standard multi-column footer. Server renders all `<details open>`
       for SEO / crawlers (all links present), then this runs client-side to
       collapse on the appropriate breakpoint.

       Uses matchMedia's change event so a resize (phone → tablet, portrait
       → landscape) re-applies the correct state instead of leaving stale
       open/closed attributes behind. */
    (function () {
      var details = document.querySelectorAll('#sks-foot-sections--21917738402006__footer .sks-foot__col-details');
      if (!details.length) return;
      var mq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 749px)');
      function apply() {
        details.forEach(function (d) {
          if (mq.matches) d.removeAttribute('open');
          else d.setAttribute('open', '');
        });
      }
      apply();
      if (mq.addEventListener) mq.addEventListener('change', apply);
      else mq.addListener(apply); /* Safari < 14 fallback */
    })();
  </script>
</div>

<script>
(function () {
  var btn = document.querySelector('[data-sks-back-to-top]');
  if (!btn) return;

  var threshold = 400;   // px scrolled before button appears
  var ticking   = false; // rAF guard so scroll handler runs at most once per frame

  function update() {
    if ((window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop) > threshold) {
      btn.classList.add('is-visible');
    } else {
      btn.classList.remove('is-visible');
    }
    ticking = false;
  }

  window.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
    if (!ticking) {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(update);
      ticking = true;
    }
  }, { passive: true });

  update();

  btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
    if ('scrollBehavior' in document.documentElement.style) {
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      window.scrollTo(0, 0);
    }
  });
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
  var KEY = 'sks_recently_viewed';
  var MAX = 24;
  // Match /products/<handle>, but exclude bare /products (the catch-all
  // listing) and any /products/<handle>/<sub> nested URLs that aren't
  // an actual product detail view.
  var m = location.pathname.match(/^\/products\/([^/?#]+)\/?$/);
  if (!m) return;
  var handle = m[1];
  if (!handle) return;
  try {
    var raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    var list = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
    if (!Array.isArray(list)) list = [];
    // De-dupe: remove any existing copy of this handle, then unshift.
    list = list.filter(function (h) { return h && h !== handle; });
    list.unshift(handle);
    if (list.length > MAX) list = list.slice(0, MAX);
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(list));
  } catch (e) {
    // Storage disabled / private mode — silently no-op.
  }
})();
</script>

  <div class="skf-mobile-bar">
    <button type="button" class="skf-mobile-back" id="skf-mobile-close" aria-label="Close search">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">

      </svg>

    <input type="search" class="skf-mobile-input" id="skf-mobile-input" placeholder="Search tartans, clans, kilts…" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false">
  </div>

<script>
(function(){
  'use strict';

  var INDEX = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('skf-search-index').textContent);
  var SHOP_DOMAIN = window.Shopify && Shopify.shop ? Shopify.shop : 'scottishkiltshop.com';
  var CDN_PREFIX = '//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/0580/7302/2627/files/';
  var POPULAR_CHIPS = ['Cameron','MacKenzie','MacDonald','Wedding kilt','Bagpipes','Sporran','DNA Finder','Burns Night'];

  /* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     NORMALISATION — Mc/Mac equivalence, typo-tolerant matching
     ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  function normalise(s){
    if (!s) return '';
    return s.toLowerCase()
      .replace(/^m[ac\u2019\'`]*\s*/, 'mac')      // m', mc, mac, m’ → mac
      .replace(/[\s\u2019\'`\-]/g, '')            // strip spaces, apostrophes, hyphens
      .trim();
  }
  function levenshtein(a, b){
    if (!a.length) return b.length;
    if (!b.length) return a.length;
    var v0 = [], v1 = [];
    for (var i = 0; i <= b.length; i++) v0[i] = i;
    for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++){
      v1[0] = i + 1;
      for (var j = 0; j < b.length; j++){
        var cost = a[i] === b[j] ? 0 : 1;
        v1[j+1] = Math.min(v1[j] + 1, v0[j+1] + 1, v0[j] + cost);
      }
      for (var j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) v0[j] = v1[j];
    }
    return v1[b.length];
  }
  function highlight(text, query){
    if (!query) return text;
    var q = query.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    return text.replace(new RegExp('(' + q + ')', 'i'), '<b>$1</b>');
  }

  /* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     SEARCH LOGIC — runs across all 5 sections in parallel
     ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  function searchAliases(q){
    var qn = normalise(q);
    var results = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < INDEX.aliases.length; i++){
      var a = INDEX.aliases[i];
      for (var t = 0; t < a.terms.length; t++){
        var tn = normalise(a.terms[t]);
        if (tn === qn || (qn.length >= 3 && tn.startsWith(qn))){
          results.push({type: a.kind, label: a.label, handle: a.h, matched: a.terms[t], score: tn === qn ? 100 : 80});
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    return results.sort(function(a,b){return b.score - a.score}).slice(0, 3);
  }

  function searchClans(q){
    var qn = normalise(q);
    if (qn.length < 2) return [];
    var results = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < INDEX.clans.length; i++){
      var c = INDEX.clans[i];
      var nn = normalise(c.n);
      if (nn === qn) results.push({clan: c, score: 100});
      else if (nn.startsWith(qn)) results.push({clan: c, score: 80});
      else if (qn.length >= 3 && nn.indexOf(qn) !== -1) results.push({clan: c, score: 50});
    }
    results.sort(function(a,b){return b.score - a.score});
    return results.slice(0, 4);
  }

  function searchSepts(q){
    var qn = normalise(q);
    if (qn.length < 2) return [];
    var results = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < INDEX.septs.length; i++){
      var s = INDEX.septs[i];
      var nn = normalise(s.n);
      if (nn === qn) results.push({sept: s, score: 100});
      else if (nn.startsWith(qn)) results.push({sept: s, score: 70});
    }
    results.sort(function(a,b){return b.score - a.score});
    return results.slice(0, 3);
  }

  /* NOTE: collections are no longer matched against the static INDEX.collections
     list — they come live from Shopify predictive search (fetchLiveSuggest +
     injectLiveCollections) so handles are always valid. The INDEX.collections
     data is left in place but unused. */

  function searchTools(q){
    var qn = normalise(q);
    var results = [];
    var triggers = {
      'tartanfinder': ['dna','ancestry','23andme','myheritage','ancestrydna','dnatest','findmytartan'],
      'septnamelookup': ['sept','surname','mysurname','myfamily'],
      'clanfinder': ['clan','clans','myclan','findmyclan'],
      'tartandesigner': ['design','customtartan','myowntartan','newtartan']
    };
    for (var i = 0; i < INDEX.tools.length; i++){
      var t = INDEX.tools[i];
      var th = normalise(t.h);
      var trigs = triggers[th] || [];
      if (th.indexOf(qn) !== -1 || trigs.some(function(x){return qn.length>=3 && x.indexOf(qn)===0 || x===qn})){
        results.push(t);
      }
    }
    return results.slice(0, 2);
  }

  /* Top products from static index, ranked by relevance */
  function searchTopProducts(q){
    var qn = normalise(q);
    if (qn.length < 2) return [];
    var results = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < INDEX.products.length; i++){
      var p = INDEX.products[i];
      var tn = normalise(p.t);
      if (tn === qn) results.push({prod: p, score: 100});
      else if (tn.indexOf(qn) !== -1) results.push({prod: p, score: 70});
    }
    results.sort(function(a,b){return b.score - a.score});
    return results.slice(0, 4);
  }

  /* Live Shopify Predictive Search — the source of truth for BOTH products
     and collections. Collections are fetched live (not from a hand-kept
     static list) so their handles are always valid: a renamed/removed
     collection can never produce a /collections/<stale-handle> 404 here,
     because we only ever render handles the store currently serves.
     One request returns both resource types. */
  function fetchLiveSuggest(q, cb){
    var url = '/search/suggest.json?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q) +
      '&resources[type]=product,collection&resources[limit]=5';
    fetch(url, {credentials:'same-origin'})
      .then(function(r){return r.ok ? r.json() : null})
      .then(function(d){
        var res = d && d.resources && d.resources.results;
        var prods = (res && res.products) ? res.products.map(function(p){
          return {title: p.title, url: p.url, image: p.image || p.featured_image || '', price: p.price};
        }) : [];
        var cols = (res && res.collections) ? res.collections.map(function(c){
          return {title: c.title, url: c.url, handle: c.handle};
        }) : [];
        cb({products: prods, collections: cols});
      })
      .catch(function(){cb({products: [], collections: []})});
  }

  /* ── Tartan catalogue (Vercel JSON snapshot) ──
     Same endpoint the product-page tartan picker uses (sks-tartan-picker.js):
     a daily-cron snapshot of the full ~5000-tartan metaobject catalogue,
     served from the Vercel edge. Loaded lazily on the first real query so
     normal page loads never pay for it; reuses the picker's localStorage
     snapshot when one exists so product-page visitors get it for free. */
  var TARTAN_API = 'https://sks-draft-order.vercel.app/api/tartans';
  var tartanCatalogue = null;
  var tartanCallbacks = [];
  var tartanFetching = false;

  function loadTartanCatalogue(cb){
    if (tartanCatalogue){ cb(tartanCatalogue); return; }
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem('sks_tartans_picker_json_v1') || sessionStorage.getItem('sks_tartans_picker_json_v1');
      if (raw){
        var c = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (c && Array.isArray(c.data) && c.data.length){
          tartanCatalogue = c.data;
          cb(tartanCatalogue);
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch(_){}
    tartanCallbacks.push(cb);
    if (tartanFetching) return;
    tartanFetching = true;
    fetch(TARTAN_API, {credentials:'omit'})
      .then(function(r){return r.ok ? r.json() : null})
      .then(function(json){
        tartanCatalogue = (json && Array.isArray(json.tartans)) ? json.tartans : [];
        var cbs = tartanCallbacks; tartanCallbacks = [];
        cbs.forEach(function(f){f(tartanCatalogue)});
      })
      .catch(function(){
        tartanCatalogue = [];
        var cbs = tartanCallbacks; tartanCallbacks = [];
        cbs.forEach(function(f){f(tartanCatalogue)});
      });
  }

  /* Top 2 tartans for the query — name matches first (exact > prefix >
     contains), then community prefix as a weak fallback. Favourites win
     ties, then shorter names (plain "MacDonald" before "MacDonald Dress"). */
  function searchTartanCatalogue(q){
    if (!tartanCatalogue || !tartanCatalogue.length) return [];
    var qn = normalise(q);
    if (qn.length < 2) return [];
    var results = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < tartanCatalogue.length; i++){
      var t = tartanCatalogue[i];
      if (!t || !t.name) continue;
      var nn = normalise(t.name);
      var score = 0;
      if (nn === qn) score = 100;
      else if (nn.indexOf(qn) === 0) score = 80;
      else if (qn.length >= 3 && nn.indexOf(qn) !== -1) score = 50;
      else if (qn.length >= 3 && normalise(t.community || '').indexOf(qn) === 0) score = 30;
      if (score) results.push({t: t, score: score});
    }
    results.sort(function(a,b){
      if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
      if (!!b.t.favourite !== !!a.t.favourite) return b.t.favourite ? 1 : -1;
      return a.t.name.length - b.t.name.length;
    });
    return results.slice(0, 2).map(function(r){return r.t});
  }

  /* Swatch images are Shopify CDN file URLs sized via ?width= (same scheme
     as the picker's imageUrlAt) — 92px ≈ 2× retina for the 46px slot. */
  function tartanSwatchUrl(url, w){
    if (!url) return '';
    url = String(url);
    if (/[?&]width=\d+/i.test(url)) return url.replace(/([?&])width=\d+/i, '$1width=' + w);
    return url + (url.indexOf('?') !== -1 ? '&' : '?') + 'width=' + w;
  }

  function buildTartanSection(q, tartans){
    if (!tartans.length) return '';
    var html = '<div class="skf-section skf-section--tartans"><div class="skf-section-h">Tartans <span class="skf-section-count">' + tartans.length + '</span></div>';
    tartans.forEach(function(t){
      // Mirrors the tartan-finder page's routing (sks-tartan-finder-page.js
      // resolveCollectionHandle + cardHtml). Each tartan record has a
      // "choose_collection" reference pointing at its parent clan collection.
      // The Vercel /api/tartans response exposes that as `collection_handle`
      // (resolved by the function) and/or `choose_collection_handle` (the
      // Storefront-resolved variant). Use whichever is present, then:
      //   • tartan.handle === collection.handle    → /collections/<handle>
      //     (this tartan IS the head of its collection — no redundant ?tartan)
      //   • tartan.handle differs (variant)        → /collections/<coll>?tartan=<handle>
      //     (the parent's collection.tartan page reads ?tartan= and renders the variant)
      //   • no parent info at all (orphan record)  → /collections/clan-pages-products-tartan?tartan=<handle>
      //     (universal fallback used by the finder page — never 404)
      var collHandle = t.collection_handle || t.choose_collection_handle || null;
      var url;
      if (collHandle && t.handle) {
        if (t.handle === collHandle) {
          url = '/collections/' + collHandle;
        } else {
          url = '/collections/' + collHandle + '?tartan=' + encodeURIComponent(t.handle);
        }
      } else if (t.choose_collection && t.handle) {
        // The tartan HAS a collection reference (a choose_collection GID) but its
        // handle didn't resolve upstream — Shopify's Admin API can't read a few
        // collections (e.g. Abel), so /api/tartans returns a null collection_handle.
        // A head tartan's collection handle equals its own handle, so route there
        // directly (/collections/<handle>) instead of the generic clan-pages
        // fallback. Only fires for these unresolved-but-referenced tartans; true
        // orphans (no choose_collection at all) still use the safe fallback below.
        url = '/collections/' + encodeURIComponent(t.handle);
      } else if (t.handle) {
        url = '/collections/clan-pages-products-tartan?tartan=' + encodeURIComponent(t.handle);
      } else {
        url = '/collections/tartan-finder?q=' + encodeURIComponent(t.name || '');
      }
      var img = tartanSwatchUrl(t.swatch_image, 92);
      var meta = [t.community, t.collection_type].filter(Boolean).join(' &middot; ');
      html += '<a class="skf-result" href="' + url + '">' +
        (img ? '<img class="skf-result-img" src="' + img + '" alt="" loading="lazy" width="46" height="46">' : '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('collection') + '</span>') +
        '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
        '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(t.name, q) + '</span>' +
        (meta ? '<span class="skf-result-meta">' + meta + '</span>' : '') +
        '</span>' +
        '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
        '</a>';
    });
    return html + '</div>';
  }

  function injectTartans(container, q){
    loadTartanCatalogue(function(){
      // Stale guard — the catalogue fetch can resolve after the user typed
      // a different query (or cleared the input). renderInto stamps the
      // container with the query it rendered; only inject if it still matches.
      if (container.dataset.skfQ !== q) return;
      container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section--tartans').forEach(function(s){s.remove()});
      var matches = searchTartanCatalogue(q);
      if (!matches.length) return;
      // "No exact matches" empty-state contradicts tartan results — drop it
      // (same rule injectLiveProducts applies for live products).
      var emptyEl = container.querySelector('.skf-empty-state');
      if (emptyEl) emptyEl.remove();
      // Placement: directly after Clans & Surnames; if no clan matched,
      // after Tools; otherwise at the top of the panel.
      var clansSec = null, toolsSec = null;
      container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function(sec){
        var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
        if (!h) return;
        if (!clansSec && /clans/i.test(h.textContent)) clansSec = sec;
        if (!toolsSec && /^\s*tools/i.test(h.textContent)) toolsSec = sec;
      });
      var anchor = clansSec || toolsSec;
      var html = buildTartanSection(q, matches);
      if (anchor) anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', html);
      else container.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', html);
    });
  }

  /* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     RENDER
     ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  function svgIcon(kind){
    var icons = {
      clan: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 2L2 7l10 5 10-5-10-5zM2 17l10 5 10-5M2 12l10 5 10-5"/></svg>',
      sept: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 00-4-4H5a4 4 0 00-4 4v2M8.5 11a4 4 0 100-8 4 4 0 000 8zM20 8v6M23 11h-6"/></svg>',
      collection: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><rect x="3" y="3" width="7" height="7"/><rect x="14" y="3" width="7" height="7"/><rect x="14" y="14" width="7" height="7"/><rect x="3" y="14" width="7" height="7"/></svg>',
      tool: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><path d="M14.7 6.3a1 1 0 000 1.4l1.6 1.6a1 1 0 001.4 0l3.77-3.77a6 6 0 01-7.94 7.94l-6.91 6.91a2.12 2.12 0 01-3-3l6.91-6.91a6 6 0 017.94-7.94l-3.76 3.76z"/></svg>',
      product: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><path d="M6 2L3 6v14a2 2 0 002 2h14a2 2 0 002-2V6l-3-4zM3 6h18M16 10a4 4 0 01-8 0"/></svg>',
      arrow: '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2"><path d="M5 12h14M13 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>',
    };
    return icons[kind] || icons.product;
  }

  function buildResults(q){
    var html = '';

    /* TOOLS — surfaces tool pages when query mentions them */
    var tools = searchTools(q);
    if (tools.length){
      html += '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Tools <span class="skf-section-count">' + tools.length + '</span></div>';
      tools.forEach(function(t){
        html += '<a class="skf-result" href="' + (t.u || '/pages/' + t.h) + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('tool') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + t.t + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">' + t.d + '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    }

    /* CLANS — surnames mapped to clan pages */
    var clans = searchClans(q);
    if (clans.length){
      html += '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Clans &amp; Surnames <span class="skf-section-count">' + clans.length + '</span></div>';
      clans.forEach(function(r){
        var c = r.clan;
        // c.s is the clan's own page handle from the index — link straight to
        // /pages/<handle> so the user lands on that clan's page, not the
        // generic clan lookup with a ?surname= query. Fall back to the old
        // query form only if a handle is somehow missing.
        var clanUrl = c.s ? '/pages/' + c.s : '/pages/clan?surname=' + encodeURIComponent(c.n);
        html += '<a class="skf-result" href="' + clanUrl + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('clan') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(c.n, q) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">View ' + c.n + ' clan history &amp; tartans</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    }

    /* SEPTS — sept name → parent clan */
    var septs = searchSepts(q);
    if (septs.length){
      html += '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Sept Names <span class="skf-section-count">' + septs.length + '</span></div>';
      septs.forEach(function(r){
        var s = r.sept;
        html += '<a class="skf-result" href="/pages/sept-name-lookup?surname=' + encodeURIComponent(s.n) + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('sept') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(s.n, q) + ' <span style="color:#A8380A;font-size:12px">&rarr;</span> ' + s.p + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">Sept of ' + s.p + (s.r ? ' &middot; ' + s.r : '') + '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    }

    /* COLLECTIONS & CATEGORIES — curated aliases render instantly here (they
       map synonyms like "wedding" or "pipes" to a chosen destination that a
       plain collection search wouldn't surface). Live Shopify collections are
       appended into this same section async by injectLiveCollections(), with
       valid handles and deduped against these aliases so the same handle can
       never appear twice. */
    var aliases = searchAliases(q);
    if (aliases.length){
      html += '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Collections &amp; Categories <span class="skf-section-count">' + aliases.length + '</span></div>';
      aliases.forEach(function(a){
        var url = a.type === 'tool' ? '/pages/' + a.handle :
                  a.type === 'product' ? '/products/' + a.handle :
                  '/collections/' + a.handle;
        html += '<a class="skf-result" href="' + url + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon(a.type) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + a.label + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">' + (a.type === 'product' ? 'Product' : a.type === 'tool' ? 'Tool page' : 'Collection') + '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    }

    /* PRODUCTS — from static index (instant) */
    var topProds = searchTopProducts(q);
    if (topProds.length){
      html += '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Products <span class="skf-section-count">' + topProds.length + '</span></div>';
      topProds.forEach(function(r){
        var p = r.prod;
        html += '<a class="skf-result" href="/products/' + p.h + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('product') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(p.t, q) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">from $' + p.p + '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      });
      html += '</div>';
    }

    return html;
  }

  function buildEmptyState(q){
    return '<div class="skf-empty-state">' +
      '<h3 class="skf-empty-h">No exact matches found</h3>' +
      '<p class="skf-empty-p">We couldn\'t find products matching <b>' + (q || '').replace(/[<>]/g,'') + '</b>. Try our heritage tools instead:</p>' +
      '<div class="skf-empty-cta">' +
      '<a href="/pages/clan?surname=' + encodeURIComponent(q) + '">Try Clan Finder</a>' +
      '<a href="/pages/sept-name-lookup?surname=' + encodeURIComponent(q) + '">Sept Name Lookup</a>' +
      '<a href="/pages/find-from-dna-results">DNA Finder</a>' +
      '<a href="/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q) + '">View all results &rarr;</a>' +
      '</div></div>';
  }

  function buildIdleState(){
    var chipsHtml = POPULAR_CHIPS.map(function(c){
      return '<button type="button" class="skf-popular-chip" data-q="' + c + '">' + c + '</button>';
    }).join('');
    return '<div class="skf-section">' +
      '<div class="skf-section-h">Try searching for</div>' +
      '<div class="skf-popular-list">' + chipsHtml + '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="skf-section">' +
      '<div class="skf-section-h">Heritage tools</div>' +
      INDEX.tools.map(function(t){
        return '<a class="skf-result" href="' + (t.u || '/pages/' + t.h) + '">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('tool') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
          '<span class="skf-result-title">' + t.t + '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-meta">' + t.d + '</span>' +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
          '</a>';
      }).join('') +
      '</div>';
  }

  /* ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
     CONTROLLER — manages 1 desktop dropdown + 1 mobile overlay
     ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  var liveCache = {};

  function renderInto(container, q){
    container.dataset.skfQ = q || '';
    if (!q || q.length < 1){
      container.innerHTML = buildIdleState();
      wirePopularChips(container);
      return;
    }
    if (q.length < 2){
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="skf-empty-state"><p class="skf-empty-p">Keep typing&hellip;</p></div>';
      return;
    }
    var html = buildResults(q);
    if (html){
      container.innerHTML = html;
    } else {
      container.innerHTML = buildEmptyState(q);
    }
    // Tartans section (top 2 from the Vercel catalogue) — async on first
    // query while the catalogue loads, instant afterwards.
    injectTartans(container, q);
    // Live enrichment — one predictive-search call feeds both the live
    // collections (into Collections & Categories) and the live products
    // (the "More products" section).
    if (liveCache[q]){
      injectLiveCollections(container, q, liveCache[q].collections);
      injectLiveProducts(container, q, liveCache[q].products);
    } else {
      fetchLiveSuggest(q, function(data){
        liveCache[q] = data;
        injectLiveCollections(container, q, data.collections);
        injectLiveProducts(container, q, data.products);
      });
    }
  }

  /* Append live Shopify collections into the Collections & Categories section.
     Live handles are always valid, so this is what eliminates the stale-404s.
     Deduped by handle against whatever is already there (curated aliases and
     earlier inject passes) so the same collection never lists twice — e.g. a
     "Sporrans" alias and the live "Sporrans" collection collapse to one row. */
  function injectLiveCollections(container, q, cols){
    // Stale-callback guard: renderInto stamps the panel with the query it
    // rendered; a late fetch for a previous query must not paint into it.
    if (container.dataset.skfQ !== q) return;
    if (!cols || !cols.length) return;

    // Locate the existing Collections & Categories section (rendered from
    // aliases). May not exist if the query matched no aliases.
    var section = null;
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function(sec){
      var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
      if (h && /collections\s*&/i.test(h.textContent)) section = sec;
    });

    // Handles already on screen — dedup source.
    var seen = {};
    (section || container).querySelectorAll('.skf-result[href*="/collections/"]').forEach(function(a){
      var m = a.getAttribute('href').match(/\/collections\/([^/?#]+)/);
      if (m) seen[m[1]] = true;
    });

    var fresh = cols.filter(function(c){ return c.handle && !seen[c.handle]; }).slice(0, 4);
    if (!fresh.length) return;

    // Live matches exist → strip any "no exact matches" empty state.
    var emptyEl = container.querySelector('.skf-empty-state');
    if (emptyEl) emptyEl.remove();

    var rows = fresh.map(function(c){
      return '<a class="skf-result" href="' + (c.url || '/collections/' + c.handle) + '">' +
        '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('collection') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
        '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(c.title, q) + '</span>' +
        '<span class="skf-result-meta">Collection</span>' +
        '</span>' +
        '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
        '</a>';
    }).join('');

    if (section){
      section.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', rows);
      var countEl = section.querySelector('.skf-section-count');
      if (countEl) countEl.textContent = section.querySelectorAll('.skf-result').length;
    } else {
      var sectionHtml = '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">Collections &amp; Categories <span class="skf-section-count">' + fresh.length + '</span></div>' + rows + '</div>';
      // Place after Septs, else after Clans, else before the first Products
      // section, else at the end — mirrors buildResults' section order.
      var anchor = null;
      container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function(sec){
        var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
        if (h && /sept/i.test(h.textContent)) anchor = sec;
      });
      if (!anchor){
        container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function(sec){
          var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
          if (h && /clans/i.test(h.textContent)) anchor = sec;
        });
      }
      if (anchor){
        anchor.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend', sectionHtml);
      } else {
        var prod = null;
        container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function(sec){
          var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
          if (!prod && h && /products/i.test(h.textContent)) prod = sec;
        });
        if (prod) prod.insertAdjacentHTML('beforebegin', sectionHtml);
        else container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', sectionHtml);
      }
    }
  }

  function injectLiveProducts(container, q, prods){
    if (!prods || !prods.length) return;

    /* ── Fix 3 — Prevent duplicate "More products" sections ──
       The function can be called more than once for the same query
       (input-event race, cache hit firing the callback again right after
       the renderInto pass already appended a section, etc.). Without
       this, every call appends a fresh "More products" section, so the
       dropdown ends up with two identical copies. Remove any existing
       More-products section before we build the new one. */
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function (sec) {
      var h = sec.querySelector('.skf-section-h');
      if (h && /more products/i.test(h.textContent)) sec.remove();
    });

    /* ── Fix 1 — Dedupe static PRODUCTS entries against live results ──
       The static index has hand-picked products with their URLs hardcoded
       at index-build time — those URLs can be stale (renamed handles,
       deleted products) and produce 404s when clicked. The live API is
       the source of truth (current URL, current price). When the same
       product appears in both, prefer the live result and remove the
       static one — even though they share a title, their URLs differ so
       the existing dedup-by-href below misses them.

       Then, if a static PRODUCTS section is left empty after removing
       its only item, drop the now-orphan section header too. */
    var liveTitleSet = {};
    prods.forEach(function (p) {
      if (p.title) liveTitleSet[String(p.title).trim().toLowerCase()] = true;
    });
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-result[href^="/products/"]').forEach(function (a) {
      var titleEl = a.querySelector('.skf-result-title');
      var t = titleEl ? titleEl.textContent.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
      if (t && liveTitleSet[t]) a.remove();
    });
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-section').forEach(function (sec) {
      if (!sec.querySelector('.skf-result')) sec.remove();
    });

    // Recount static products AFTER deduplication, so we know whether
    // there's still room for live ones.
    var staticProductCount = container.querySelectorAll('.skf-result[href^="/products/"]').length;
    if (staticProductCount >= 4) return;  // already plenty

    // Filter out products we already showed (URL-level dedup, catches
    // anything that survived the title-level dedup above).
    var existingHrefs = {};
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-result[href^="/products/"]').forEach(function(a){existingHrefs[a.getAttribute('href')] = true});
    var fresh = prods.filter(function(p){return !existingHrefs[p.url]}).slice(0, 5 - staticProductCount);
    if (!fresh.length) return;

    /* ── Fix 2 — Strip the empty-state when we have results to show ──
       buildEmptyState() renders "No exact matches found" with recovery
       links when the STATIC index has zero matches. But the live API can
       still find products for the same query — when it does, the empty
       state contradicts the results below it ("No matches" + a list of
       2 matching products in the same panel). Remove the empty-state
       block before we append our results so they don't compete. */
    var emptyStateEl = container.querySelector('.skf-empty-state');
    if (emptyStateEl) emptyStateEl.remove();

    var html = '<div class="skf-section"><div class="skf-section-h">More products <span class="skf-section-count">' + fresh.length + '</span></div>';
    fresh.forEach(function(p){
      // Price: show the live suggest price as-is. When it's $0 (outfit-builder
      // products priced at 0), leave an empty slot tagged with the handle and
      // fill it from the product's custom.card_price metafield via ?view=price
      // — so the dropdown matches the cards without changing anything else.
      var _zero = (p.price == null || p.price === '' || parseFloat(String(p.price).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) === 0);
      var _priceHtml = _zero
        ? '<span class="skf-result-meta skf-result-price" data-skf-price="' + skfHandleFromUrl(p.url) + '"></span>'
        : '<span class="skf-result-meta skf-result-price">' + formatPredictivePrice(p.price) + '</span>';
      html += '<a class="skf-result" href="' + p.url + '">' +
        (p.image ? '<img class="skf-result-img" src="' + thumbifyImage(p.image) + '" alt="" loading="lazy" width="46" height="46">' : '<span class="skf-result-icon">' + svgIcon('product') + '</span>') +
        '<span class="skf-result-body">' +
        '<span class="skf-result-title">' + highlight(p.title, q) + '</span>' +
        _priceHtml +
        '</span>' +
        '<span class="skf-result-arrow">' + svgIcon('arrow') + '</span>' +
        '</a>';
    });
    html += '</div>';
    container.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
    skfFillMetaPrices(container);
  }

  /* For $0-priced results, pull the display price from the product's
     custom.card_price metafield via the tiny ?view=price template. Cached by
     handle so repeated keystrokes never re-fetch; only outfit ($0) products
     ever trigger a request, and if the metafield is empty the slot stays blank
     (never shows "$0.00"). */
  var skfPriceCache = {};
  function skfHandleFromUrl(url){
    var m = /\/products\/([^/?#]+)/.exec(url || '');
    return m ? m[1] : '';
  }
  function skfFillMetaPrices(scope){
    var nodes = scope.querySelectorAll('[data-skf-price]');
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(nodes, function(el){
      var h = el.getAttribute('data-skf-price');
      if (!h) return;
      if (skfPriceCache[h] != null) { el.innerHTML = skfPriceCache[h]; return; }
      fetch('/products/' + encodeURIComponent(h) + '?view=price', {credentials:'same-origin'})
        .then(function(r){ return r.ok ? r.text() : ''; })
        .then(function(t){ t = (t || '').trim(); skfPriceCache[h] = t; el.innerHTML = t; })
        .catch(function(){});
    });
  }

  /* Shopify Predictive Search returns price either as a formatted string
     ("$139.00") or a numeric string in dollars ("139.00") — never cents.
     The previous (p.price / 100) was producing $1.39 for $139 products.
     This helper normalises both shapes to a "$XX.XX" output. */
  function formatPredictivePrice(price){
    if (price == null || price === '') return '';
    if (typeof price === 'string' && /[^\d.]/.test(price)) return price; // already has currency symbol
    var n = parseFloat(price);
    if (isNaN(n)) return '';
    return '$' + n.toFixed(2);
  }

  /* Replace a Shopify CDN image URL with its small (92px) thumbnail so the
     dropdown doesn't pull a 2000×3000 master JPEG just to render a 46×46
     square. Works whether the URL already has a size suffix, has none, or
     carries query params. Falls back to the original URL if it doesn't
     match the Shopify CDN pattern (e.g. external images). */
  function thumbifyImage(url){
    if (!url) return '';
    if (url.indexOf('cdn.shopify.com') === -1 && url.indexOf('cdn.shopifycdn.net') === -1) return url;
    var qIdx = url.indexOf('?');
    var path = qIdx === -1 ? url : url.substring(0, qIdx);
    // Strip any existing _SIZE. suffix before the extension.
    path = path.replace(/_(pico|icon|thumb|small|compact|medium|large|grande|original|master|\d+x\d*)\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp|gif)$/i, '.$2');
    // Insert _92x. (92px wide ≈ 2× retina for a 46×46 slot).
    path = path.replace(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp|gif)(\?|$)/i, '_92x.$1$2');
    return qIdx === -1 ? path : path + url.substring(qIdx);
  }

  function wirePopularChips(container){
    container.querySelectorAll('.skf-popular-chip').forEach(function(chip){
      chip.addEventListener('click', function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        // stopPropagation is critical: renderInto() below replaces the
        // panel's innerHTML, which DETACHES this chip from the DOM. If we
        // let the click bubble to the document-level "click outside shell"
        // close handler (attached in attachDesktop), it checks
        // shell.contains(e.target) — but e.target is now a detached node
        // and that returns false, so the panel gets hidden the instant
        // after it renders the results.
        e.stopPropagation();
        var q = chip.dataset.q;
        // Don't rely on activeInput() — clicking the chip moves focus off
        // the search input, so document.activeElement is no longer the
        // input by the time this handler fires. Walk up to the
        // .skf-search-shell that wraps both the panel (container) and the
        // input, then pick the input by selector. Falls back to the
        // mobile overlay's input for the mobile path which reuses the
        // same chip markup.
        var shell = chip.closest('.skf-search-shell');
        var input = shell ? shell.querySelector('input[type="search"]') :
                            document.getElementById('skf-mobile-input');
        if (!input) return;
        input.value = q;
        input.focus();
        renderInto(container, q);
      });
    });
  }

  /* ────── Desktop: attach to all <input type="search"> ────── */
  function attachDesktop(input){
    if (input.dataset.skfAttached) return;
    if (input.closest('.skf-mobile-overlay')) return; // skip mobile overlay's own input
    input.dataset.skfAttached = '1';
    input.setAttribute('autocomplete','off');

    // Wrap input in shell to position panel relatively
    var shell = document.createElement('div');
    shell.className = 'skf-search-shell';
    input.parentNode.insertBefore(shell, input);
    shell.appendChild(input);

    var panel = document.createElement('div');
    panel.className = 'skf-search-panel';
    panel.setAttribute('role','listbox');
    shell.appendChild(panel);

    var debounceTimer;
    function showPanel(){
      panel.classList.add('skf-open');
      document.getElementById('skf-backdrop').classList.add('skf-open');
    }
    function hidePanel(){
      panel.classList.remove('skf-open');
      document.getElementById('skf-backdrop').classList.remove('skf-open');
    }

    input.addEventListener('focus', function(){
      showPanel();
      renderInto(panel, input.value.trim());
    });
    input.addEventListener('input', function(){
      var q = input.value.trim();
      clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
      debounceTimer = setTimeout(function(){
        showPanel();
        renderInto(panel, q);
      }, 120);
    });

    // Close on outside click
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (!shell.contains(e.target)) hidePanel();
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && panel.classList.contains('skf-open')){
        hidePanel();
        input.blur();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('skf-backdrop').addEventListener('click', hidePanel);
  }

  /* ────── Mobile overlay ────── */
  function setupMobile(){
    var trigger = document.getElementById('skf-mobile-trigger');
    var overlay = document.getElementById('skf-mobile-overlay');
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('skf-mobile-close');
    var input = document.getElementById('skf-mobile-input');
    var results = document.getElementById('skf-mobile-results');
    if (!overlay) return;

    // Track the scrollY so we can restore it after close. The scroll-lock
    // class pins <html> and <body> with overflow:hidden + touch-action:
    // none, which iOS Safari respects (unlike body.style.overflow alone
    // which can still be broken by touch-drag on inner scroll containers).
    var _lockedScrollY = 0;
    function open(){
      _lockedScrollY = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
      overlay.classList.add('skf-open');
      document.documentElement.classList.add('skf-body-locked');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      setTimeout(function(){input.focus()}, 100);
      renderInto(results, '');
    }
    function close(){
      overlay.classList.remove('skf-open');
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('skf-body-locked');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      // Restore scroll position in case iOS Safari nudged it during lock.
      window.scrollTo(0, _lockedScrollY);
    }

    /* Theme-provided trigger(s): any element with [data-skf-mobile-open]
       is wired to open the overlay. This is the preferred path — the SKS
       header places its own search icon next to the cart icon, styled to
       match the surrounding nav icons. When the theme provides one, we
       use it directly and DON'T inject the snippet's own auto-trigger
       into the page so there's only one icon visible. */
    var externalTriggers = document.querySelectorAll('[data-skf-mobile-open]');
    if (externalTriggers.length){
      externalTriggers.forEach(function(el){
        el.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); open(); });
      });
      // Hide the snippet's auto-trigger completely — theme owns the icon now.
      if (trigger) trigger.style.display = 'none';
    } else if (trigger){
      // Fallback: no theme trigger found — auto-inject into the nav like before.
      var navTarget = document.querySelector('header nav, .header__icons, [class*="header"] [class*="icons"], header .actions, header [class*="action"]');
      if (navTarget && window.innerWidth <= 768){
        navTarget.insertBefore(trigger, navTarget.firstChild);
        trigger.classList.add('skf-show');
      } else {
        trigger.style.position = 'fixed';
        trigger.style.top = '12px';
        trigger.style.right = '60px';
        trigger.style.zIndex = '999';
        trigger.classList.add('skf-show');
      }
      trigger.addEventListener('click', open);
    }

    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', close);

    var debTimer;
    input.addEventListener('input', function(){
      clearTimeout(debTimer);
      var q = input.value.trim();
      debTimer = setTimeout(function(){renderInto(results, q)}, 120);
    });
    input.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.key === 'Enter'){
        e.preventDefault();
        var firstResult = results.querySelector('.skf-result');
        if (firstResult) firstResult.click();
        else if (input.value.trim()) window.location.href = '/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(input.value.trim());
      }
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('skf-open')) close();
    });
  }

  /* ────── INIT ────── */
  function activeInput(){
    return document.activeElement && document.activeElement.tagName === 'INPUT' ? document.activeElement : null;
  }

  function init(){
    // OPT-IN ONLY: only inputs that explicitly carry the .skf-enhance
    // class get the dropdown. The bundled snippet's original behaviour
    // was to attach to every input[type="search"] on the page, which
    // would have hijacked the inner-tartan product search, the tartan
    // finder page filter, the clan finder search, the sept lookup
    // input, and the homepage tartan picker — every one of those does
    // its own page-local filtering and would break under the dropdown.
    // Scoping by class lets each page decide whether to enable the
    // dropdown explicitly. Header search opts in; everything else is
    // untouched.
    var SELECTOR = 'input[type="search"].skf-enhance';

    document.querySelectorAll(SELECTOR).forEach(attachDesktop);
    // Watch for inputs added later (mobile drawer, etc.) — same scope.
    var mo = new MutationObserver(function(muts){
      muts.forEach(function(m){
        m.addedNodes.forEach(function(node){
          if (node.nodeType !== 1) return;
          if (node.matches && node.matches(SELECTOR)) attachDesktop(node);
          if (node.querySelectorAll) node.querySelectorAll(SELECTOR).forEach(attachDesktop);
        });
      });
    });
    mo.observe(document.body, {childList: true, subtree: true});

    setupMobile();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }

  /* Public API — exposes the search functions so other sections can render
     the same categorized results inline (e.g. the /search results page
     wants to show Clans / Septs / Collections / Tools above its product
     grid). Kept minimal: just buildResults() and a ready flag so callers
     don't render before the snippet has parsed its index data. */
  window.skfSearch = {
    ready: true,
    INDEX: INDEX,
    buildResults: buildResults
  };
  document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('skf-search:ready'));
})();
</script>
<script>
    (function () {
      var INTERNAL = [
        'scottishkiltshop.com',
        'cdn.shopify.com',
        'cdn.shop.app'
      ];

      function isInternal(host) {
        return INTERNAL.some(function (d) {
          return host === d || host.endsWith('.' + d);
        });
      }

      function apply(root) {
        var links = root.querySelectorAll('a[href^="http"]:not([data-nf])');
        links.forEach(function (a) {
          a.setAttribute('data-nf', '1');
          var host;
          try { host = new URL(a.href).hostname; }
          catch (e) { return; }
          if (isInternal(host)) return;
          var rel = (a.getAttribute('rel') || '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
          ['nofollow', 'noopener', 'noreferrer'].forEach(function (t) {
            if (rel.indexOf(t) === -1) rel.push(t);
          });
          a.setAttribute('rel', rel.join(' '));
          if (!a.target) a.target = '_blank';
        });
      }

      apply(document);
      new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
        muts.forEach(function (m) {
          m.addedNodes.forEach(function (n) {
            if (n.nodeType === 1) apply(n);
          });
        });
      }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    })();
    </script>

    <script>
    (function () {
      function ensureScriptOnce(src, id) {
        if (id && document.getElementById(id)) return;
        if (id) {
          var existing = document.querySelector('script[src="' + src + '"]');
          if (existing) return;
        }
        var script = document.createElement('script');
        if (id) script.id = id;
        script.setAttribute('src', src);
        document.body.appendChild(script);
      }

      function scheduleEditorReload(srcBase) {
        // In Theme Editor, changing settings replaces section DOM, but the
        // Reviews.io script may not automatically re-scan. Reload it (debounced).
        if (!window.__REVIEWS_EDITOR__) return;
        if (window.__reviewsioFloatingReloadTimer) {
          window.clearTimeout(window.__reviewsioFloatingReloadTimer);
        }
        window.__reviewsioFloatingReloadTimer = window.setTimeout(function () {
          var script = document.createElement('script');
          script.setAttribute('src', srcBase + '?editorReload=' + Date.now());
          script.setAttribute('data-reviewsio-editor-reload', 'true');
          document.body.appendChild(script);
        }, 150);
      }

      var __reviewsioFloatingInitAttempts = 0;
      var MAX_INIT_ATTEMPTS = 25;
      var INIT_RETRY_DELAY_MS = 200;

      function init() {
        var fallbackStore = (window.Shopify && typeof window.Shopify.shop === 'string') ? window.Shopify.shop : '';
        var storeFromCore = (typeof reviewsIoStore !== 'undefined' && reviewsIoStore) ? reviewsIoStore : '';
        var store = storeFromCore || fallbackStore;
        if (!store) {
          __reviewsioFloatingInitAttempts += 1;
          if (__reviewsioFloatingInitAttempts < MAX_INIT_ATTEMPTS) {
            window.setTimeout(init, INIT_RETRY_DELAY_MS);
          }
          return;
        }

        var widgets = document.querySelectorAll('.reviews-io-floating-widget');
        if (!widgets || !widgets.length) return;

        var hasAnyValidWidgetId = false;
        var shouldReloadInEditor = false;
        widgets.forEach(function (widget) {
          var widgetId = (widget.getAttribute('data-widget-id') || '').trim();
          if (!widgetId) return;
          hasAnyValidWidgetId = true;

          var currentStore = (widget.getAttribute('data-store-name') || '').trim();

          // Avoid overriding demo preview injection (demo widget id + demo store).
          var demo = window.__REVIEWS_DEMO_CONFIG__;
          var demoWidgetId = demo && demo.widgetIds ? demo.widgetIds.floating : '';
          var isDemoInjected = !!(demo && demo.store && demoWidgetId && widgetId === demoWidgetId && currentStore === demo.store);

          if (!currentStore) {
            widget.setAttribute('data-store-name', store);
            currentStore = (widget.getAttribute('data-store-name') || '').trim();
          } else if (!isDemoInjected && storeFromCore && currentStore !== storeFromCore) {
            widget.setAttribute('data-store-name', storeFromCore);
            currentStore = (widget.getAttribute('data-store-name') || '').trim();
          }

          if (typeof reviewsIoLang !== 'undefined' && reviewsIoLang) {
            widget.setAttribute('lang', reviewsIoLang);
          }

          if (window.__REVIEWS_EDITOR__) {
            var lang = (typeof reviewsIoLang !== 'undefined' && reviewsIoLang) ? reviewsIoLang : '';
            var key = widgetId + '|' + currentStore + '|' + lang;
            var prevKey = widget.getAttribute('data-reviewsio-init-key') || '';
            if (prevKey !== key) {
              widget.setAttribute('data-reviewsio-init-key', key);
              shouldReloadInEditor = true;
            }
          }
        });

        if (!hasAnyValidWidgetId) return;

        if (window.__REVIEWS_EDITOR__) {
          // If settings changed, re-run the script so it re-scans the page.
          if (shouldReloadInEditor) {
            scheduleEditorReload('https://widget.reviews.io/modern-widgets/floating.js');
          } else {
            ensureScriptOnce('https://widget.reviews.io/modern-widgets/floating.js', 'reviewsio-floating-js');
          }
          return;
        }

        ensureScriptOnce('https://widget.reviews.io/modern-widgets/floating.js', 'reviewsio-floating-js');
      }

      window.__reviewsioInitFloatingWidgets = window.__reviewsioInitFloatingWidgets || init;

      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
      } else {
        init();
      }

      document.addEventListener('shopify:section:load', init);
      document.addEventListener('shopify:block:select', init);
    })();
    </script>
  

</reviewsio-floating>
